| E-XE164-018 | VERIFIZIERT | The bounded C166 L2 receive path and ARM L3 rows 7–9 encode complementary static transfer steps: ARM row 7 writes a four-byte byte-swapped L3 length and expects the same four bytes, row 8 writes raw L3 bytes, and row 9 writes their one-byte XOR and expects `0x81`. C166 L2 reads four bytes, echoes them, receives the nonzero low-word byte count into segment `E0` from offset `0x0200` while XOR-accumulating, compares the next byte, sends `0x81` on equality and jumps to `E0:0200`; on mismatch it sends `0x7e` and returns to the four-byte receive step. | L2 SHA-256 `b4a7eddb...79245`; ARM ELF SHA-256 `3b7b7ca4...bc5d6`; FW30 IPK SHA-256 `f06340a7...0fe` | L2 REA/Rizin `ev_27597860110883533afe19a60dfabad2ca8bc9be0c9f79e3ed296258375be40e` plus Ghidra listing SHA-256 `52bee366...d299`; ARM pseudocode `ev_ff77a7...17cc` and table bytes `ev_a47d2d...81b6`; root cross-side Self-Review | This verifies matching static sender/receiver mechanics for the zero-first-word/nonzero-second-word path and the initial ARM table values.
